# PacEdu Class 3 English — 460 Daily Printable Worksheets
**Status:** PacEdu draft only. `PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW`; `PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION`. Not a Fiji Ministry-issued worksheet pack.
115 days × four separate Copper/Bronze/Silver/Gold versions. Select one day and one pathway in the viewer. Local date/time comes from the school's configured IANA timezone; missing configuration must be visibly flagged. The approved school calendar determines teaching days.
Learner: __________ School: __________ Teacher: __________ Level: Class 3
Accept appropriate accessible response modes. Credit accurate synonyms and paraphrases. Do not penalise accent or disability-related speech differences. Use only taught content and authorised class texts.

---

## Day 1 · Term 1 Week 1 Day 1 · Copper
**Theme:** Our stories and identities | **Area:** Listen and speak
**Objective:** Tell or read a personal/community story; identify sequence, detail and viewpoint; create a short narrative.
**PacEdu working achievement indicator:** Learner can listen actively, take turns, ask a relevant question and give a clear response.
**Task:** Listen to a short prompt, take a turn, ask a relevant question and respond clearly. Prompt: “What is one important idea about our stories and identities? Show or explain how you know.” Use today's authorised class text or teacher-created prompt.
**Copper:** Model each step; use short chunks, word bank and visual/tactile cues. Accept sign, AAC, pointing, speech, drawing or dictation.
**Worked example:** Model: identify the question → find a relevant idea/detail → plan → respond → check that the answer matches the prompt. Teacher models with the actual class text.
**Learner response:** ________________________________________________
**Answer guidance:** Credit a relevant answer supported by the text or taught content; accept equivalent wording. For interpretation, credit a different view when supported by evidence.
**Evidence/next step:** ____________________  **Indicator/source page:** PENDING VERIFICATION  **Review:** PENDING TEACHER REVIEW

**Objective:** Summarise an environmental text; select evidence; create a persuasive message.
**PacEdu working achievement indicator:** Learner can read or view a level-appropriate text; identify purpose, key ideas and supporting details.
**Task:** Read a level-appropriate text; find the main idea and two supporting details. Prompt: “What is one important idea about oceans and environmental care? Show or explain how you know.” Use today's authorised class text or teacher-created prompt.
**Gold:** Extend the same task with comparison, a reason, counter-view or precise detail; keep the shared objective.
**Worked example:** Model: identify the question → find a relevant idea/detail → plan → respond → check that the answer matches the prompt. Teacher models with the actual class text.
**Learner response:** ________________________________________________
**Answer guidance:** Credit a relevant answer supported by the text or taught content; accept equivalent wording. For interpretation, credit a different view when supported by evidence.
**Evidence/next step:** ____________________  **Indicator/source page:** PENDING VERIFICATION  **Review:** PENDING TEACHER REVIEW

## Day 114 · Term 3 Week 5 Day 4 · Copper
**Theme:** Oceans and environmental care | **Area:** Think about the text
**Objective:** Summarise an environmental text; select evidence; create a persuasive message.
**PacEdu working achievement indicator:** Learner can examine how structure, language and viewpoint shape meaning; support an observation.
**Task:** Explain a viewpoint, language choice or text feature using an example. Prompt: “What is one important idea about oceans and environmental care? Show or explain how you know.” Use today's authorised class text or teacher-created prompt.
**Copper:** Model each step; use short chunks, word bank and visual/tactile cues. Accept sign, AAC, pointing, speech, drawing or dictation.
**Worked example:** Model: identify the question → find a relevant idea/detail → plan → respond → check that the answer matches the prompt. Teacher models with the actual class text.
**Learner response:** ________________________________________________
**Answer guidance:** Credit a relevant answer supported by the text or taught content; accept equivalent wording. For interpretation, credit a different view when supported by evidence.
